// =========================================================
// FILE: js/boss.js
// NAVE YORHA + ESTELAS DE VELOCIDAD (BLUR) + ESPADAS CLAVADAS + EMBESTIDAS
// =========================================================
const BOSS_AUDIO_PATHS = {
    bgmPhase1: "audio/bgm/boss-music-fase1.mp3",
    bgmPhase2: "audio/bgm/boss-music-fase2.mp3",
    bgmPhase3: "audio/bgm/boss-music-fase3.mp3",
    bossIntro: "audio/sfx/superattack.mp3",
    bossDefeat: "audio/bgm/ending.mp3",
    vineBoom: "audio/sfx/boom.mp3",
    defeatLaugh: "audio/sfx/defeatlaught.mp3",
    crystalSpawn: "audio/sfx/crystalbreak.mp3",
    crystalBreak: "audio/sfx/crystalbreak.mp3",
    trapHeartSpawn: "audio/sfx/trapspawn.mp3",
    trapHeartHit: "audio/sfx/traphit.mp3",
    enrageAlarm: "audio/sfx/enragealarm.mp3",
    sealBreak: "audio/sfx/sealbreak.mp3",
    antiSpamBlock: "audio/sfx/antispamblock.mp3",
    hitBoss: "audio/sfx/hitboss.mp3",
    bombExplode: "audio/sfx/bombexplode.mp3",
    bellSound: "audio/sfx/bell.mp3",
    rewindSound: "audio/sfx/rewind.mp3",
    ecoOrbital: "audio/sfx/ecoorbital.mp3",
    endingBgm: "audio/bgm/ending.mp3"
};

const isMobile = window.innerWidth <= 680;
const BOSS_SPRITES = {
    normal: "img/portraits/cabezanormal.png",
    angry: "img/portraits/cabezaenojada.png",
    yandere: "img/portraits/cabezayandere.png",
    corePhase1_2: "img/boss/core.png",
    corePhase3_pc: "img/boss/boos_fase3.gif",
    corePhase3_mobile: "img/boss/boss_fase3.png"
};

const BossState = {
    phase: 1, maxHp: 480, currentHp: 480, playerHp: 3, maxPlayerHp: 3,
    damageLock: true, isInvulnerable: false, weaponLost: false,
    history: [], battleStartTime: 0, hpAtStart: 0,

    // Nave YoRHa (WASD / Joystick)
    shipX: window.innerWidth / 2,
    shipY: window.innerHeight * 0.78,
    shipSpeed: 385,
    keys: { w: false, a: false, s: false, d: false },
    joyDx: 0,
    joyDy: 0,

    // Auto-Ataque (1 punto de daño por bala)
    playerBullets: [],
    fireCooldown: 0,
    fireRate: 0.085,
    hitsSinceLastMove: 0,
    lastPopupTime: 0,
    aggroRange: 360,

    // Escudo en Barra Espaciadora
    shieldActive: false,
    shieldTimer: 0,
    shieldDuration: 2.3,
    shieldCooldown: 0,
    shieldMaxCooldown: 4.3,
    playerIFrames: 0,

    // Railgun (25 de daño)
    railgunCharge: 0,
    railgunBeamTimer: 0,
    railgunTargetX: 0,
    railgunTargetY: 0,

    // Efectos de Velocidad (Afterimages / Motion Blur), Espadas Clavadas y Embestidas (Tackle)
    afterimages: [],
    lastCoreX: window.innerWidth / 2,
    lastCoreY: window.innerHeight * 0.3,
    impaledSwords: [],
    tackleState: 'idle', // 'idle', 'warn', 'charge'
    tackleTimer: 0,
    tackleOriginX: 0,
    tackleOriginY: 0,
    tackleTargetX: 0,
    tackleTargetY: 0,
    tackleHasHit: false,

    recordEvent(type, x, y) {
        if (this.phase < 3) {
            this.history.push({ time: Date.now() - this.battleStartTime, type, x, y, drawn: false });
        }
    },

    getBossCoreCenter() {
        const core = document.getElementById('boss-core');
        if (core && core.style.display !== 'none') {
            const r = core.getBoundingClientRect();
            return { x: r.left + r.width / 2, y: r.top + r.height / 2, radius: r.width * 0.42, kind: 'core' };
        }
        return { x: window.innerWidth / 2, y: window.innerHeight * 0.30, radius: 55, kind: 'core' };
    },

    getPriorityTarget() {
        let bestNode = null;
        let minDist = this.aggroRange;

        for (let i = 0; i < BossAttacks.nodes.length; i++) {
            const n = BossAttacks.nodes[i];
            const d = Math.hypot(n.x - this.shipX, n.y - this.shipY);
            if (d <= minDist) {
                minDist = d;
                bestNode = n;
            }
        }
        if (bestNode) {
            return { x: bestNode.x, y: bestNode.y, radius: bestNode.radius, kind: 'node', ref: bestNode };
        }

        for (let i = 0; i < BossAttacks.clones.length; i++) {
            const c = BossAttacks.clones[i];
            if (c.x && c.y) {
                const d = Math.hypot(c.x - this.shipX, c.y - this.shipY);
                if (d <= 300) {
                    return { x: c.x, y: c.y, radius: 42, kind: 'clone', ref: c };
                }
            }
        }

        return this.getBossCoreCenter();
    },

    // --- EMBESTIDA FÍSICA DE SAPHYNIEL (TACKLE / DASH) ---
    startBossTackle() {
        const core = document.getElementById('boss-core');
        if (!core || core.style.display === 'none' || this.tackleState !== 'idle' || this.currentHp <= 0) return;

        const cPos = this.getBossCoreCenter();
        this.tackleOriginX = cPos.x;
        this.tackleOriginY = cPos.y;
        this.tackleTargetX = Math.max(80, Math.min(window.innerWidth - 80, this.shipX));
        this.tackleTargetY = Math.max(100, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 80, this.shipY));
        this.tackleState = 'warn';
        this.tackleTimer = 0.62;
        this.tackleHasHit = false;

        if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.beep(520, 0.18, 'sawtooth', 0.045);
    },

    updateBossTackleAndBlur(dt, ctx) {
        const core = document.getElementById('boss-core');
        const coreImg = document.getElementById('boss-core-img');
        if (!core || core.style.display === 'none') return;

        const curPos = this.getBossCoreCenter();
        const moveDist = Math.hypot(curPos.x - this.lastCoreX, curPos.y - this.lastCoreY);

        // Si Saphyniel se está desplazando rápido, generar estelas fantasma (Afterimages / Blur)
        if (moveDist > 4.5 && coreImg) {
            this.afterimages.push({
                x: curPos.x,
                y: curPos.y,
                size: curPos.radius * 2.1,
                alpha: 0.48,
                img: coreImg
            });
        }
        this.lastCoreX = curPos.x;
        this.lastCoreY = curPos.y;

        // Dibujar Afterimages (Estelas de velocidad estilo NieR)
        for (let i = this.afterimages.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const af = this.afterimages[i];
            af.alpha -= dt * 1.9;
            if (af.alpha <= 0) {
                this.afterimages.splice(i, 1);
                continue;
            }
            ctx.save();
            ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, af.alpha);
            ctx.translate(af.x, af.y);
            try {
                ctx.drawImage(af.img, -af.size / 2, -af.size / 2, af.size, af.size);
            } catch (e) {}
            ctx.restore();
        }

        // Actualizar máquina de estados de la Embestida (Tackle)
        if (this.tackleState === 'warn') {
            this.tackleTimer -= dt;
            ctx.save();
            ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(217, 56, 30, ${0.45 + Math.sin(performance.now() * 0.03) * 0.35})`;
            ctx.lineWidth = 4;
            ctx.setLineDash([12, 8]);
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(curPos.x, curPos.y);
            ctx.lineTo(this.tackleTargetX, this.tackleTargetY);
            ctx.stroke();

            ctx.strokeStyle = '#e5c158';
            ctx.setLineDash([]);
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(this.tackleTargetX, this.tackleTargetY, 34, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.stroke();
            ctx.restore();

            if (this.tackleTimer <= 0) {
                this.tackleState = 'charge';
                this.tackleTimer = 0.38;
                core.classList.add('tackle-mode');
                if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.swordImpactSound();
            }
        } else if (this.tackleState === 'charge') {
            this.tackleTimer -= dt;
            const progress = Math.min(1, 1 - (this.tackleTimer / 0.38));
            const ease = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3);
            const nx = this.tackleOriginX + (this.tackleTargetX - this.tackleOriginX) * ease;
            const ny = this.tackleOriginY + (this.tackleTargetY - this.tackleOriginY) * ease;

            core.style.left = `${(nx / window.innerWidth) * 100}%`;
            core.style.top = `${(ny / window.innerHeight) * 100}%`;

            // Colisión física del placaje contra la nave del jugador
            const distToShip = Math.hypot(nx - this.shipX, ny - this.shipY);
            if (!this.tackleHasHit && distToShip < curPos.radius + 22) {
                this.tackleHasHit = true;
                if (this.shieldActive) {
                    // ¡PARRY CLASH CON EL ESCUDO!
                    ParticleEngine.explode(this.shipX, this.shipY, '#e5c158', 30);
                    if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.railgunFireSound();
                    if (typeof ScreenUtils !== 'undefined') ScreenUtils.triggerShake();
                    if (typeof BalatroEngine !== 'undefined') BalatroEngine.triggerHit(this.shipX, this.shipY, 1.8);
                    this.spawnDamagePopup(this.shipX, this.shipY - 25, "¡EMBESTIDA BLOQUEADA!", 'blocked');
                } else {
                    this.tryHitPlayer(this.shipX, this.shipY);
                }
            }

            if (this.tackleTimer <= 0) {
                this.tackleState = 'idle';
                core.classList.remove('tackle-mode');
                // Al terminar la embestida, regresa suavemente a la zona superior
                setTimeout(() => { if (this.tackleState === 'idle') this.moveBossCore(); }, 220);
            }
        }

        // Dibujar todas las Espadas (swordsprite.png) que están clavadas en el cuerpo de Saphyniel
        if (this.impaledSwords.length > 0) {
            BossAttacks.initAssets();
            const sImg = BossAttacks.swordImg;
            this.impaledSwords.forEach(sw => {
                ctx.save();
                ctx.translate(curPos.x + sw.offsetX, curPos.y + sw.offsetY);
                ctx.rotate(sw.angle + Math.PI / 2);
                if (sImg && sImg.complete && sImg.naturalWidth > 0) {
                    ctx.shadowColor = '#f7f5ed';
                    ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
                    ctx.drawImage(sImg, -15, -40, 30, 80);
                } else {
                    ctx.fillStyle = '#f7f5ed';
                    ctx.fillRect(-3, -35, 6, 55);
                }
                ctx.restore();
            });
        }
    },

    activateShield() {
        if (this.shieldActive || this.shieldCooldown > 0 || this.playerHp <= 0) return;
        this.shieldActive = true;
        this.shieldTimer = this.shieldDuration;
        this.shieldCooldown = this.shieldMaxCooldown;

        if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.evadeOverclockSound();
        if (typeof ParticleEngine !== 'undefined') {
            ParticleEngine.explode(this.shipX, this.shipY, '#dad4bb', 16);
        }
        this.spawnDamagePopup(this.shipX, this.shipY - 28, "ESCUDO YORHA", 'heal');
    },

    activateRailgun() {
        if (this.playerHp <= 0) return;
        // Si perdimos el Railgun en la Fase 3, presionar E invoca una Espada del Juicio desde las esquinas
        if (this.weaponLost && this.phase === 3 && !this.damageLock) {
            BossAttacks.spawnJudgmentSword(null, 12);
            return;
        }
        if (this.weaponLost || this.damageLock || this.railgunCharge < 100) return;

        this.railgunCharge = 0;
        this.railgunBeamTimer = 0.45;
        const target = this.getPriorityTarget();
        this.railgunTargetX = target.x;
        this.railgunTargetY = target.y;

        if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') {
            AudioManager.railgunFireSound();
            AudioManager.playAudio(BOSS_AUDIO_PATHS.ecoOrbital, 0.55);
        }
        if (typeof ScreenUtils !== 'undefined') ScreenUtils.triggerShake();
        if (typeof BalatroEngine !== 'undefined') BalatroEngine.triggerHit(target.x, target.y, 1.5);
        if (typeof ParticleEngine !== 'undefined') ParticleEngine.explode(target.x, target.y, '#e5c158', 30);

        BossAttacks.bullets = [];

        if (target.kind === 'node' && target.ref) {
            const idx = BossAttacks.nodes.indexOf(target.ref);
            if (idx !== -1) BossAttacks.nodes.splice(idx, 1);
            this.dealDamageToBoss(25, target.x, target.y, true);
        } else {
            this.dealDamageToBoss(25, target.x, target.y, true);
        }
    },

    dealDamageToBoss(dmg, hitX, hitY, isRailgun = false) {
        if (this.damageLock || this.weaponLost) return;

        this.currentHp = Math.max(1, this.currentHp - dmg);
        this.recordEvent('hit', hitX, hitY);
        this.updateHpBar();

        const now = performance.now();
        if (isRailgun || now - this.lastPopupTime > 190) {
            this.lastPopupTime = now;
            this.spawnDamagePopup(hitX, hitY, isRailgun ? `RAILGUN -${dmg}` : dmg, isRailgun ? 'blocked' : 'normal');
        }

        this.hitsSinceLastMove += dmg;
        if (this.hitsSinceLastMove >= 24 && this.tackleState === 'idle') {
            this.hitsSinceLastMove = 0;
            this.moveBossCore();
        }

        BossEngine.handleHit(dmg, { x: hitX, y: hitY });
    },

    updateAndRenderShip(dt, ctx) {
        if (this.playerHp <= 0) return;

        // Renderizar estelas de velocidad (Blur) y embestidas de Saphyniel
        this.updateBossTackleAndBlur(dt, ctx);

        // 1. Movimiento WASD + Joystick móvil
        let vx = this.joyDx;
        let vy = this.joyDy;
        if (this.keys.w) vy -= 1;
        if (this.keys.s) vy += 1;
        if (this.keys.a) vx -= 1;
        if (this.keys.d) vx += 1;

        if (vx !== 0 || vy !== 0) {
            const len = Math.hypot(vx, vy);
            if (len > 1) { vx /= len; vy /= len; }
            this.shipX += vx * this.shipSpeed * dt;
            this.shipY += vy * this.shipSpeed * dt;
        }

        const margin = 26;
        this.shipX = Math.max(margin, Math.min(window.innerWidth - margin, this.shipX));
        this.shipY = Math.max(margin + 60, Math.min(window.innerHeight - margin - 20, this.shipY));

        // 2. Actualizar Escudo y Railgun
        const blessing = localStorage.getItem('codex_active_blessing');
        const cooldownMult = blessing === 'silver' ? 1.3 : 1.0;

        if (this.playerIFrames > 0) this.playerIFrames = Math.max(0, this.playerIFrames - dt);
        if (this.shieldActive) {
            this.shieldTimer -= dt;
            if (this.shieldTimer <= 0) this.shieldActive = false;
        } else if (this.shieldCooldown > 0) {
            this.shieldCooldown = Math.max(0, this.shieldCooldown - dt * cooldownMult);
        }

        if (!this.damageLock && !this.weaponLost && this.railgunCharge < 100) {
            this.railgunCharge = Math.min(100, this.railgunCharge + dt * 14 * cooldownMult);
        }
        this.updateSkillHudBars();

        // 3. Fijación de Blanco con Prioridad de Aggro
        const target = this.getPriorityTarget();
        const aimAngle = Math.atan2(target.y - this.shipY, target.x - this.shipX);

        if (!this.weaponLost && !this.damageLock && (target.kind === 'node' || target.kind === 'clone')) {
            ctx.save();
            ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(218, 212, 187, 0.4)';
            ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
            ctx.setLineDash([6, 6]);
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(this.shipX, this.shipY);
            ctx.lineTo(target.x, target.y);
            ctx.stroke();

            ctx.strokeStyle = '#e5c158';
            ctx.setLineDash([]);
            ctx.strokeRect(target.x - 28, target.y - 28, 56, 56);
            ctx.restore();
        }

        this.fireCooldown -= dt;
        const effectiveFireRate = blessing === 'crimson' ? this.fireRate * 0.72 : this.fireRate;
        if (this.fireCooldown <= 0 && !this.damageLock && !this.weaponLost && this.playerHp > 0) {
            this.fireCooldown = effectiveFireRate;
            const spread = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 10;
            const perpX = -Math.sin(aimAngle) * spread;
            const perpY = Math.cos(aimAngle) * spread;

            this.playerBullets.push({
                x: this.shipX + Math.cos(aimAngle) * 18 + perpX,
                y: this.shipY + Math.sin(aimAngle) * 18 + perpY,
                vx: Math.cos(aimAngle) * 16,
                vy: Math.sin(aimAngle) * 16
            });
            if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined' && Math.random() < 0.3) {
                AudioManager.podFireSound();
            }
        }

        // 4. Actualizar balas de la nave (Paleta Marfil/Ámbar YoRHa)
        for (let i = this.playerBullets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const pb = this.playerBullets[i];
            pb.x += pb.vx * (dt * 60);
            pb.y += pb.vy * (dt * 60);

            if (pb.x < -20 || pb.x > window.innerWidth + 20 || pb.y < -20 || pb.y > window.innerHeight + 20) {
                this.playerBullets.splice(i, 1);
                continue;
            }

            ctx.save();
            ctx.fillStyle = '#f7f5ed';
            ctx.shadowColor = '#dad4bb';
            ctx.shadowBlur = 8;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(pb.x, pb.y, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.restore();

            // A. Colisión contra Bombas o Cruces
            let hitNode = false;
            for (let j = BossAttacks.nodes.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
                const n = BossAttacks.nodes[j];
                if (Math.hypot(pb.x - n.x, pb.y - n.y) < n.radius + 6) {
                    n.hp -= 1;
                    this.railgunCharge = Math.min(100, this.railgunCharge + 1.8);
                    ParticleEngine.explode(pb.x, pb.y, '#e5c158', 2);
                    if (n.hp <= 0) {
                        ParticleEngine.explode(n.x, n.y, '#dad4bb', 18);
                        if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.beep(880, 0.08, 'sine', 0.05);
                        this.spawnDamagePopup(n.x, n.y, n.type === 'bomb' ? "DESACTIVADA" : "SELLO ROTO", 'heal');
                        BossAttacks.nodes.splice(j, 1);
                    }
                    hitNode = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (hitNode) {
                this.playerBullets.splice(i, 1);
                continue;
            }

            // B. Colisión contra Clones
            let hitClone = false;
            for (let j = BossAttacks.clones.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
                const c = BossAttacks.clones[j];
                if (c.x && c.y && Math.hypot(pb.x - c.x, pb.y - c.y) < 42) {
                    c.hp -= 1;
                    ParticleEngine.explode(pb.x, pb.y, '#d9381e', 2);
                    if (c.hp <= 0) {
                        ParticleEngine.explode(c.x, c.y, '#d9381e', 18);
                        if (c.el) c.el.remove();
                        BossAttacks.clones.splice(j, 1);
                    }
                    hitClone = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (hitClone) {
                this.playerBullets.splice(i, 1);
                continue;
            }

            // C. Colisión contra el Núcleo de Saphyniel
            const corePos = this.getBossCoreCenter();
            if (Math.hypot(pb.x - corePos.x, pb.y - corePos.y) < corePos.radius) {
                this.playerBullets.splice(i, 1);
                this.railgunCharge = Math.min(100, this.railgunCharge + 1.4);
                ParticleEngine.explode(pb.x, pb.y, '#dad4bb', 2);
                this.dealDamageToBoss(1, pb.x, pb.y, false);
            }
        }

        // 5. Dibujar haz del Railgun
        if (this.railgunBeamTimer > 0) {
            this.railgunBeamTimer -= dt;
            ctx.save();
            const beamWidth = 34 * (this.railgunBeamTimer / 0.45);
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#e5c158';
            ctx.lineWidth = beamWidth;
            ctx.shadowColor = '#f7f5ed';
            ctx.shadowBlur = 30;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(this.shipX, this.shipY);
            ctx.lineTo(this.railgunTargetX, this.railgunTargetY);
            ctx.stroke();

            ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
            ctx.lineWidth = beamWidth * 0.45;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(this.shipX, this.shipY);
            ctx.lineTo(this.railgunTargetX, this.railgunTargetY);
            ctx.stroke();
            ctx.restore();
        }

        // 6. Dibujar la Nave YoRHa y su Escudo (Paleta NieR:Automata)
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(this.shipX, this.shipY);

        if (this.shieldActive) {
            ctx.save();
            ctx.rotate(performance.now() * 0.004);
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#f7f5ed';
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(218, 212, 187, 0.22)';
            ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
            ctx.shadowColor = '#dad4bb';
            ctx.shadowBlur = 18;
            ctx.beginPath();
            for (let s = 0; s < 6; s++) {
                const a = (Math.PI * 2 * s) / 6;
                const rx = Math.cos(a) * 35;
                const ry = Math.sin(a) * 35;
                if (s === 0) ctx.moveTo(rx, ry);
                else ctx.lineTo(rx, ry);
            }
            ctx.closePath();
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.stroke();
            ctx.restore();
        }

        ctx.rotate(aimAngle + Math.PI / 2);

        // Propulsores Marfil / Carmesí
        ctx.fillStyle = this.weaponLost ? '#d9381e' : '#f7f5ed';
        ctx.shadowColor = this.weaponLost ? '#d9381e' : '#dad4bb';
        ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(-7, 14);
        ctx.lineTo(0, 24 + Math.random() * 8);
        ctx.lineTo(7, 14);
        ctx.fill();

        // Casco YoRHa
        ctx.fillStyle = this.weaponLost ? '#8b8778' : '#dad4bb';
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#f7f5ed';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, -20);
        ctx.lineTo(16, 14);
        ctx.lineTo(6, 9);
        ctx.lineTo(0, 15);
        ctx.lineTo(-6, 9);
        ctx.lineTo(-16, 14);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.stroke();

        ctx.fillStyle = this.weaponLost ? '#d9381e' : (this.railgunCharge >= 100 ? '#e5c158' : '#09090b');
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(0, 0, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        ctx.restore();
    },

    updateSkillHudBars() {
        const shieldFill = document.getElementById('shield-bar-fill');
        const mobShieldBtn = document.getElementById('mob-shield-btn');
        const mobShieldState = document.getElementById('mob-shield-state');

        if (this.shieldActive) {
            const pct = (this.shieldTimer / this.shieldDuration) * 100;
            if (shieldFill) shieldFill.style.width = `${pct}%`;
            if (mobShieldBtn) mobShieldBtn.classList.add('active');
            if (mobShieldState) mobShieldState.textContent = "ACTIVO";
        } else if (this.shieldCooldown > 0) {
            const pct = ((this.shieldMaxCooldown - this.shieldCooldown) / this.shieldMaxCooldown) * 100;
            if (shieldFill) shieldFill.style.width = `${pct}%`;
            if (mobShieldBtn) mobShieldBtn.classList.remove('active');
            if (mobShieldState) mobShieldState.textContent = `${this.shieldCooldown.toFixed(1)}s`;
        } else {
            if (shieldFill) shieldFill.style.width = '100%';
            if (mobShieldBtn) mobShieldBtn.classList.remove('active');
            if (mobShieldState) mobShieldState.textContent = "LISTO";
        }

        const railFill = document.getElementById('railgun-bar-fill');
        const railLabel = document.getElementById('railgun-label');
        const railBox = document.getElementById('hud-railgun-btn');
        const mobRailBtn = document.getElementById('mob-railgun-btn');
        const mobRailState = document.getElementById('mob-railgun-state');

        if (this.weaponLost) {
            if (railBox) {
                railBox.classList.remove('ready');
                railBox.classList.add('disabled-weapon');
            }
            if (railLabel) railLabel.textContent = "ESPADAS DE FE [E]";
            if (mobRailBtn) mobRailBtn.classList.add('ready');
            if (mobRailState) mobRailState.textContent = "ESPADA";
            return;
        }

        if (railBox) railBox.classList.remove('disabled-weapon');
        const rPct = Math.floor(this.railgunCharge);
        if (railFill) railFill.style.width = `${rPct}%`;
        if (railLabel) railLabel.textContent = rPct >= 100 ? "RAILGUN [E] ¡LISTO!" : `RAILGUN [E] ${rPct}%`;
        if (railBox) railBox.classList.toggle('ready', rPct >= 100);
        if (mobRailBtn) mobRailBtn.classList.toggle('ready', rPct >= 100);
        if (mobRailState) mobRailState.textContent = rPct >= 100 ? "¡DISPARAR!" : `${rPct}%`;
    },

    tryHitPlayer(x, y) {
        if (this.shieldActive) {
            this.spawnDamagePopup(this.shipX, this.shipY - 20, "BLOQUEADO", 'heal');
            if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.beep(880, 0.06, 'sine', 0.04);
            return;
        }
        if (this.playerIFrames > 0) return;
        this.playerIFrames = 1.15;
        this.damagePlayer();
    },

    resetBossEncounter() {
        BossAttacks.clearAll();
        if (BossEngine.currentPhase && typeof BossEngine.currentPhase.exit === 'function') {
            BossEngine.currentPhase.exit();
        }

        const blessing = localStorage.getItem('codex_active_blessing');
        this.maxPlayerHp = blessing === 'gold' ? 4 : 3;
        this.playerHp = this.maxPlayerHp;
        this.shipX = window.innerWidth / 2;
        this.shipY = window.innerHeight * 0.78;
        this.playerBullets = [];
        this.afterimages = [];
        this.impaledSwords = [];
        this.tackleState = 'idle';
        this.railgunCharge = 50;
        this.shieldActive = false;
        this.shieldCooldown = 0;
        this.weaponLost = false;

        document.body.className = 'combat-no-cursor';
        document.getElementById('cinematic-darkness')?.classList.remove('active');

        const core = document.getElementById('boss-core');
        if (core) {
            core.style.display = 'flex';
            core.style.opacity = '1';
            core.style.top = '30%';
            core.style.left = '50%';
            core.style.transform = `translate(-50%, -50%) scale(${isMobile ? 1.0 : 1.45})`;
            core.className = 'boss-core-container';
        }

        const coreImg = document.getElementById('boss-core-img');
        if (coreImg) {
            coreImg.className = 'boss-core-img';
            coreImg.src = BOSS_SPRITES.corePhase1_2;
        }

        const bossHud = document.getElementById('boss-hud');
        if (bossHud) { bossHud.style.display = 'flex'; bossHud.style.opacity = '1'; }

        const playerHud = document.getElementById('player-hud');
        if (playerHud) { playerHud.style.display = 'flex'; playerHud.style.opacity = '1'; }

        const heartsRow = document.getElementById('hearts-row');
        if (heartsRow) {
            heartsRow.innerHTML = Array.from({ length: this.maxPlayerHp }, (_, idx) =>
                `<span class="player-heart" id="heart-${idx + 1}">❤</span>`
            ).join('');
        }

        document.getElementById('boss-shield')?.classList.remove('active', 'divine');
        document.getElementById('boss-aura-farm')?.classList.remove('active', 'max-power');
        document.getElementById('ending-screen')?.classList.remove('visible');
        document.getElementById('game-over-screen')?.classList.remove('visible');
        document.getElementById('end-record-final')?.classList.remove('active');
        const lyrics = document.getElementById('lyrics-container');
        if (lyrics) lyrics.innerHTML = '';
    },

    updateCoreSprite() {
        const img = document.getElementById('boss-core-img');
        if (!img) return;
        if (this.phase === 3) {
            img.src = isMobile ? BOSS_SPRITES.corePhase3_mobile : BOSS_SPRITES.corePhase3_pc;
        } else if (this.phase === 2) {
            img.src = "img/boss/ddddboss_fase2.png";
            img.onerror = () => { img.src = BOSS_SPRITES.corePhase1_2; };
        } else {
            img.src = BOSS_SPRITES.corePhase1_2;
        }
    },

    updateHpBar() {
        const pct = Math.max(0, Math.round((this.currentHp / this.maxHp) * 100));
        const fill = document.getElementById('boss-hp-fill');
        const num = document.getElementById('boss-hp-num');
        if (fill) fill.style.width = `${pct}%`;
        if (num) num.textContent = `[${pct}%]`;
        if (typeof BalatroEngine !== 'undefined') {
            BalatroEngine.setBossHpRatio(this.currentHp / this.maxHp);
        }
    },

    spawnDamagePopup(x, y, dmg, type = 'normal') {
        const el = document.createElement('div');
        el.className = `damage-number ${type}`;
        if (type === 'heal') el.textContent = typeof dmg === 'number' ? `+${dmg}` : dmg;
        else if (type === 'blocked') el.textContent = dmg;
        else el.textContent = `-${dmg}`;
        el.style.left = `${x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 35}px`;
        el.style.top = `${y + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20}px`;
        document.body.appendChild(el);
        setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 550);
    },

    damagePlayer() {
        if (this.playerHp <= 0) return;
        this.playerHp--;
        if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.beep(90, 0.3, 'sawtooth', 0.08);
        if (typeof ScreenUtils !== 'undefined') ScreenUtils.triggerShake();
        for (let i = 1; i <= this.maxPlayerHp; i++) {
            const heart = document.getElementById(`heart-${i}`);
            if (heart) heart.classList.toggle('lost', i > this.playerHp);
        }
        if (this.playerHp <= 0) {
            BossAttacks.clearAll();
            this.triggerBadEnding();
        }
    },

    triggerBadEnding() {
        BossState.damageLock = true;
        BossAttacks.clearAll();
        AudioManager.stopBgm();

        if (BossEngine.currentPhase && typeof BossEngine.currentPhase.exit === 'function') {
            BossEngine.currentPhase.exit();
        }

        document.body.className = '';
        window.parent.postMessage({ type: 'UNLOCK_PSALMS_REROLL' }, '*');

        document.getElementById('boss-core').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('boss-hud').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('player-hud').style.display = 'none';

        AudioManager.playAudio(BOSS_AUDIO_PATHS.defeatLaugh);
        document.getElementById('game-over-screen')?.classList.add('visible');
        document.getElementById('retry-btn')?.classList.add('visible');
    },

    moveBossCore() {
        const core = document.getElementById('boss-core');
        if (!core || this.tackleState !== 'idle') return;
        const targetX = isMobile ? Math.random() * 50 + 25 : Math.random() * 62 + 19;
        const targetY = isMobile ? Math.random() * 24 + 18 : Math.random() * 28 + 16;

        // Pulso sutil en el shader al desplazarse
        const cPos = this.getBossCoreCenter();
        if (typeof BalatroEngine !== 'undefined') {
            BalatroEngine.triggerHit(cPos.x, cPos.y, 0.55);
        }

        core.style.top = `${targetY}%`;
        core.style.left = `${targetX}%`;
        this.recordEvent('move', targetX, targetY);
        if (BossAttacks.clones.length > 0) BossAttacks.moveClones();
    }
};

window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
    if (k === 'w' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') BossState.keys.w = true;
    if (k === 'a' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') BossState.keys.a = true;
    if (k === 's' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') BossState.keys.s = true;
    if (k === 'd' || e.key === 'ArrowRight') BossState.keys.d = true;

    if (e.code === 'Space') {
        e.preventDefault();
        BossState.activateShield();
    }
    if (k === 'e' || k === 'q' || e.key === 'Enter') {
        e.preventDefault();
        BossState.activateRailgun();
    }
});

window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
    const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
    if (k === 'w' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') BossState.keys.w = false;
    if (k === 'a' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') BossState.keys.a = false;
    if (k === 's' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') BossState.keys.s = false;
    if (k === 'd' || e.key === 'ArrowRight') BossState.keys.d = false;
});

(function initMobileControls() {
    const zone = document.getElementById('joystick-zone');
    const stick = document.getElementById('joystick-stick');
    const shieldBtn = document.getElementById('mob-shield-btn');
    const railBtn = document.getElementById('mob-railgun-btn');

    if (shieldBtn) {
        shieldBtn.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();
            BossState.activateShield();
        });
    }
    if (railBtn) {
        railBtn.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();
            BossState.activateRailgun();
        });
    }

    if (!zone || !stick) return;
    let activeTouchId = null;
    const maxRadius = 40;

    const handleMove = (clientX, clientY) => {
        const rect = zone.getBoundingClientRect();
        const cx = rect.left + rect.width / 2;
        const cy = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
        let dx = clientX - cx;
        let dy = clientY - cy;
        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        if (dist > maxRadius) {
            dx = (dx / dist) * maxRadius;
            dy = (dy / dist) * maxRadius;
        }
        stick.style.transform = `translate(${dx}px, ${dy}px)`;
        BossState.joyDx = dx / maxRadius;
        BossState.joyDy = dy / maxRadius;
    };

    const resetJoystick = () => {
        activeTouchId = null;
        stick.style.transform = 'translate(0px, 0px)';
        BossState.joyDx = 0;
        BossState.joyDy = 0;
    };

    zone.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const t = e.changedTouches[0];
        activeTouchId = t.identifier;
        handleMove(t.clientX, t.clientY);
    }, { passive: false });

    zone.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        for (let i = 0; i < e.changedTouches.length; i++) {
            if (e.changedTouches[i].identifier === activeTouchId) {
                handleMove(e.changedTouches[i].clientX, e.changedTouches[i].clientY);
                break;
            }
        }
    }, { passive: false });

    zone.addEventListener('touchend', resetJoystick);
    zone.addEventListener('touchcancel', resetJoystick);
})();

function initBossEncounterSequence() {
    window.focus();
    BossState.resetBossEncounter();
    BossState.battleStartTime = Date.now();
    BossState.history = [];
    BossEngine.startLoop();

    const debugParam = BossEngine.checkDebugParams();
    if (debugParam) {
        if (debugParam.startsWith('3')) BossEngine.transitionTo('phase3');
        else if (debugParam === '2') BossEngine.transitionTo('phase2');
        else BossEngine.transitionTo('phase1');
    } else {
        BossEngine.transitionTo('phase1');
    }
}

document.getElementById('choice-yes')?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    document.getElementById('p5-choices')?.classList.remove('active');
    DialogueSystem.show({
        text: "Al menos fuiste honesto. Gracias por no disfrazar tu indiferencia.",
        speaker: "SAPHYNIEL",
        tone: "mid",
        autoHideTime: 3000,
        spriteMap: BOSS_SPRITES,
        callback: () => {
            setTimeout(() => {
                window.parent.postMessage({ type: 'BOSS_DEFEATED', ending: 'HONEST_EXIT' }, '*');
            }, 800);
        }
    });
});

document.getElementById('choice-no')?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    document.getElementById('p5-choices')?.classList.remove('active');
    document.body.classList.add('combat-no-cursor');
    DialogueSystem.show({
        text: "Seguiste disparando hasta el final. No finjas piedad ahora que ya no puedo defenderme.",
        speaker: "SAPHYNIEL",
        tone: "yandere",
        autoHideTime: 3000,
        spriteMap: BOSS_SPRITES,
        callback: () => {
            document.getElementById('cinematic-darkness')?.classList.add('active');
            BossEngine.transitionTo('phase3');
        }
    });
});

document.getElementById('retry-btn')?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    initBossEncounterSequence();
});

window.addEventListener('message', (e) => {
    if (e.data && e.data.type === 'START_BOSS') {
        if (typeof BalatroEngine !== 'undefined' && !BalatroEngine.animId) {
            BalatroEngine.init('balatro-canvas');
        }
        initBossEncounterSequence();
    }
});

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    if (typeof BalatroEngine !== 'undefined') BalatroEngine.init('balatro-canvas');
    if (typeof ParticleEngine !== 'undefined') ParticleEngine.init('particle-canvas');
    if (window.parent === window) {
        setTimeout(initBossEncounterSequence, 500);
    }
});
=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: js/boss/boss-attacks.js ===
// =========================================================
// SISTEMA DE ATAQUES YORHA: ESPIRALES, MURO, RED LÁSER,
// ESPADAS DEL JUICIO (swordsprite.png) Y EMBESTIDAS FÍSICAS
// =========================================================
const BossAttacks = {
    qteSpawnerTimer: null,
    bombSpawnerTimer: null,
    danmakuSpawnerTimer: null,
    laserSpawnerTimer: null,
    patternSpawnerTimer: null,
    survivalTimer: null,
    clones: [],
    bullets: [],
    lasers: [],
    nodes: [],
    faithOrbs: [],
    judgmentSwords: [], // Espadas lanzadas desde las 4 esquinas hacia Saphyniel
    spiralAngle: 0,
    swordImg: null,

    initAssets() {
        if (!this.swordImg) {
            this.swordImg = new Image();
            this.swordImg.src = "img/icons/swordsprite.png";
        }
    },

    getNonOverlappingCoords(marginVw = 18, marginVh = 22) {
        const left = Math.floor(Math.random() * (100 - marginVw * 2)) + marginVw;
        const top = Math.floor(Math.random() * (100 - marginVh * 2)) + marginVh;
        return { left, top };
    },

    // --- 1. PROYECTILES CON PALETA AUTÉNTICA NIER:AUTOMATA ---
    // Carmesí Termonuclear (#d9381e / #ff9e80) y Obsidiana Violácea (#1c0d26 / #8a2848)
    spawnDanmakuRing(originX, originY, count = 12, speed = 3.2, pattern = 'ring') {
        if (BossState.damageLock && BossState.phase !== 3) return;
        const isMob = window.innerWidth <= 680;
        const finalCount = isMob ? Math.max(6, Math.floor(count * 0.7)) : count;
        const baseAngle = Math.atan2(BossState.shipY - originY, BossState.shipX - originX);

        for (let i = 0; i < finalCount; i++) {
            const angle = pattern === 'aimed'
                ? baseAngle + (i - (finalCount - 1) / 2) * 0.18
                : (Math.PI * 2 * i) / finalCount + (performance.now() * 0.0012);
            const isLight = i % 2 === 0;
            this.bullets.push({
                x: originX,
                y: originY,
                vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
                vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
                radius: isMob ? 8.5 : 10,
                coreColor: isLight ? '#d9381e' : '#1c0d26',
                ringColor: isLight ? '#ff9e80' : '#b8325a',
                centerColor: isLight ? '#f7f5ed' : '#dad4bb'
            });
        }
        if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.beep(240, 0.04, 'sawtooth', 0.02);
    },

    // NUEVO ATAQUE: Espiral de Relojería (Clockwork Spiral)
    spawnSpiralVolley(arms = 3, bulletsPerArm = 2, speed = 3.4) {
        if (BossState.damageLock && BossState.phase !== 3) return;
        const core = BossState.getBossCoreCenter();
        this.spiralAngle += 0.38;

        for (let a = 0; a < arms; a++) {
            const armAngle = this.spiralAngle + (Math.PI * 2 * a) / arms;
            for (let k = 0; k < bulletsPerArm; k++) {
                const spd = speed + k * 0.65;
                const isLight = (a + k) % 2 === 0;
                this.bullets.push({
                    x: core.x,
                    y: core.y,
                    vx: Math.cos(armAngle) * spd,
                    vy: Math.sin(armAngle) * spd,
                    radius: 9.5,
                    coreColor: isLight ? '#d9381e' : '#1c0d26',
                    ringColor: isLight ? '#ff9e80' : '#b8325a',
                    centerColor: '#f7f5ed'
                });
            }
        }
    },

    // NUEVO ATAQUE: Ráfaga de Francotirador en Cono (Aimed Tri-Burst)
    spawnAimedBurst() {
        if (BossState.damageLock && BossState.phase !== 3) return;
        for (let w = 0; w < 3; w++) {
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (BossState.playerHp <= 0 || (BossState.damageLock && BossState.phase !== 3)) return;
                const core = BossState.getBossCoreCenter();
                this.spawnDanmakuRing(core.x, core.y, 5, 4.3 + w * 0.5, 'aimed');
            }, w * 180);
        }
    },

    // NUEVO ATAQUE: Muro de Cortina con Hueco (Curtain Wall)
    spawnCurtainWall() {
        if (BossState.damageLock && BossState.phase !== 3) return;
        const total = window.innerWidth <= 680 ? 11 : 18;
        const gapIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * (total - 4)) + 2;
        const stepX = window.innerWidth / total;

        for (let i = 0; i <= total; i++) {
            if (Math.abs(i - gapIndex) <= 1) continue; // Brecha para pasar con la nave
            this.bullets.push({
                x: i * stepX,
                y: 65,
                vx: 0,
                vy: 2.85,
                radius: 9.5,
                coreColor: '#1c0d26',
                ringColor: '#d9381e',
                centerColor: '#dad4bb'
            });
        }
        if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.beep(190, 0.12, 'sawtooth', 0.03);
    },

    // NUEVO ATAQUE: Red de Láseres Cruzados (Sacred Grid)
    spawnLaserGrid(count = 2) {
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            setTimeout(() => this.spawnTelegraphLaser(), i * 240);
        }
    },

    spawnTelegraphLaser() {
        if (BossState.damageLock && !BossState.weaponLost) return;
        const yPos = BossState.shipY + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 160;
        const angle = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.65;
        this.lasers.push({
            x: window.innerWidth / 2,
            y: Math.max(110, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 110, yPos)),
            angle,
            state: 'warn',
            timer: 0,
            warnDuration: 1.05,
            fireDuration: 0.38,
            hasDamaged: false
        });
        if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.beep(880, 0.09, 'triangle', 0.025);
    },

    // --- 2. ESPADAS DEL JUICIO (img/icons/swordsprite.png) DESDE LAS 4 ESQUINAS ---
    // El sprite tiene el mango abajo y la hoja hacia arriba (^), por lo que rotamos atan2(dy, dx) + Math.PI / 2
    spawnJudgmentSword(cornerIndex = null, damage = 14) {
        if (BossState.phase !== 3 || BossState.currentHp <= 0) return;
        this.initAssets();

        const w = window.innerWidth;
        const h = window.innerHeight;
        const corners = [
            { x: -30, y: -30 },
            { x: w + 30, y: -30 },
            { x: -30, y: h + 30 },
            { x: w + 30, y: h + 30 }
        ];
        const c = cornerIndex !== null ? corners[cornerIndex % 4] : corners[Math.floor(Math.random() * 4)];
        const core = BossState.getBossCoreCenter();
        const angle = Math.atan2(core.y - c.y, core.x - c.x);

        this.judgmentSwords.push({
            x: c.x,
            y: c.y,
            speed: 19,
            angle,
            damage
        });

        if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.beep(1120, 0.06, 'sawtooth', 0.03);
    },

    // Orbes de Fe dorados en Fase 3 para recargar el Escudo al tocarlos
    spawnFaithOrb() {
        const coords = this.getNonOverlappingCoords(20, 25);
        this.faithOrbs.push({
            x: (coords.left / 100) * window.innerWidth,
            y: (coords.top / 100) * window.innerHeight,
            radius: 13,
            timer: 6.5
        });
    },

    // --- 3. BOMBAS Y CRUCES DE CURACIÓN CON PRIORIDAD DE AGGRO ---
    spawnBomb(isGolden = false) {
        if (BossState.playerHp <= 0) return;
        const coords = this.getNonOverlappingCoords(18, 24);
        const x = (coords.left / 100) * window.innerWidth;
        const y = (coords.top / 100) * window.innerHeight;
        const hp = BossState.phase === 3 ? 6 : 9;

        this.nodes.push({
            type: 'bomb',
            x, y,
            hp, maxHp: hp,
            radius: 26,
            timer: 4.3,
            maxTimer: 4.3,
            isGolden
        });
    },

    spawnQteCross() {
        if (BossState.playerHp <= 0) return;
        const coords = this.getNonOverlappingCoords(18, 24);
        const x = (coords.left / 100) * window.innerWidth;
        const y = (coords.top / 100) * window.innerHeight;
        const hp = 9;

        this.nodes.push({
            type: 'cross',
            x, y,
            hp, maxHp: hp,
            radius: 26,
            timer: 4.8,
            maxTimer: 4.8
        });
        // Anillo escolta lento alrededor de la cruz de curación
        this.spawnDanmakuRing(x, y, 6, 1.8, 'ring');
    },

    spawnHeartTrap() {
        this.spawnQteCross();
    },

    // --- 4. CLONES DE ILUSIÓN ATACANTES ---
    spawnClones() {
        this.clearClones();
        for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
            const coords = this.getNonOverlappingCoords(20, 22);
            const el = document.createElement('div');
            el.className = 'boss-core-container boss-clone-container spawn-blip';
            el.style.opacity = '0.55';
            el.style.filter = 'sepia(80%) hue-rotate(-30deg) contrast(135%)';
            el.style.left = `${coords.left}%`;
            el.style.top = `${coords.top}%`;
            el.innerHTML = `
                <div class="sacred-wheel-1"></div>
                <div class="boss-core-btn">
                    <img class="boss-core-img" src="img/boss/ddddboss_fase2.png" onerror="this.src='img/boss/core.png'" alt="Clone">
                </div>
            `;
            document.body.appendChild(el);
            this.clones.push({
                el,
                left: coords.left,
                top: coords.top,
                hp: 26,
                maxHp: 26,
                fireTimer: 2.0 + i * 1.3
            });
        }
    },

    moveClones() {
        this.clones.forEach(c => {
            if (c.el && document.body.contains(c.el)) {
                const coords = this.getNonOverlappingCoords(20, 22);
                c.left = coords.left;
                c.top = coords.top;
                c.el.style.left = `${coords.left}%`;
                c.el.style.top = `${coords.top}%`;
            }
        });
    },

    clearClones() {
        this.clones.forEach(c => { if (c.el) c.el.remove(); });
        this.clones = [];
    },

    updateProjectilesAndLasers(dt, ctx) {
        if (!ctx) return;
        this.initAssets();

        // A. Actualizar Clones Atacantes
        for (let i = this.clones.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const c = this.clones[i];
            if (!c.el || !document.body.contains(c.el)) {
                this.clones.splice(i, 1);
                continue;
            }
            const r = c.el.getBoundingClientRect();
            c.x = r.left + r.width / 2;
            c.y = r.top + r.height / 2;

            if (!BossState.damageLock) {
                c.fireTimer -= dt;
                if (c.fireTimer <= 0) {
                    c.fireTimer = 3.8;
                    this.spawnDanmakuRing(c.x, c.y, 4, 2.5, 'aimed');
                }
            }
        }

        // B. Actualizar Espadas del Juicio (swordsprite.png) volando hacia Saphyniel
        const core = BossState.getBossCoreCenter();
        for (let i = this.judgmentSwords.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const sw = this.judgmentSwords[i];
            // Guiado dinámico hacia la posición actual del GIF de Saphyniel
            sw.angle = Math.atan2(core.y - sw.y, core.x - sw.x);
            sw.x += Math.cos(sw.angle) * sw.speed * (dt * 60);
            sw.y += Math.sin(sw.angle) * sw.speed * (dt * 60);

            // Dibujar estela dorada y el sprite swordsprite.png rotado (punta hacia arriba = angle + Math.PI / 2)
            ctx.save();
            ctx.translate(sw.x, sw.y);
            ctx.rotate(sw.angle + Math.PI / 2);

            if (this.swordImg && this.swordImg.complete && this.swordImg.naturalWidth > 0) {
                ctx.shadowColor = '#f7f5ed';
                ctx.shadowBlur = 16;
                ctx.drawImage(this.swordImg, -18, -48, 36, 96);
            } else {
                // Fallback vectorial si el sprite tarda en cargar
                ctx.fillStyle = '#f7f5ed';
                ctx.shadowColor = '#e5c158';
                ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.moveTo(0, -45);
                ctx.lineTo(7, 15);
                ctx.lineTo(-7, 15);
                ctx.closePath();
                ctx.fill();
                ctx.fillRect(-16, 15, 32, 5);
                ctx.fillRect(-4, 20, 8, 18);
            }
            ctx.restore();

            // Al impactar a Saphyniel, se clava físicamente en su cuerpo y le resta vida
            if (Math.hypot(sw.x - core.x, sw.y - core.y) < core.radius * 0.78) {
                ParticleEngine.explode(sw.x, sw.y, '#f7f5ed', 14);
                ParticleEngine.explode(sw.x, sw.y, '#d9381e', 8);
                if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.swordImpactSound();
                if (typeof ScreenUtils !== 'undefined') ScreenUtils.triggerShake();

                // Clavar la espada en el cuerpo de Saphyniel (máximo 18 espadas visibles clavadas)
                if (BossState.impaledSwords.length < 18) {
                    const impactDist = 28 + Math.random() * 24;
                    BossState.impaledSwords.push({
                        angle: sw.angle,
                        offsetX: -Math.cos(sw.angle) * impactDist,
                        offsetY: -Math.sin(sw.angle) * impactDist
                    });
                }

                BossState.currentHp = Math.max(1, BossState.currentHp - sw.damage);
                BossState.updateHpBar();
                BossState.spawnDamagePopup(core.x, core.y, sw.damage, 'normal');
                this.judgmentSwords.splice(i, 1);
            }
        }

        // C. Actualizar Orbes de Fe (Recargan el Escudo al tocarlos con la nave)
        for (let i = this.faithOrbs.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const orb = this.faithOrbs[i];
            orb.timer -= dt;
            if (orb.timer <= 0) {
                this.faithOrbs.splice(i, 1);
                continue;
            }
            ctx.save();
            ctx.translate(orb.x, orb.y);
            ctx.rotate(performance.now() * 0.004);
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#e5c158';
            ctx.fillStyle = '#f7f5ed';
            ctx.shadowColor = '#e5c158';
            ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
            ctx.strokeRect(-9, -9, 18, 18);
            ctx.fillRect(-4, -4, 8, 8);
            ctx.restore();

            if (Math.hypot(BossState.shipX - orb.x, BossState.shipY - orb.y) < 34) {
                BossState.shieldCooldown = 0;
                ParticleEngine.explode(orb.x, orb.y, '#e5c158', 12);
                if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.beep(1046, 0.08, 'sine', 0.05);
                BossState.spawnDamagePopup(orb.x, orb.y, "ESCUDO RECARGADO", 'heal');
                this.faithOrbs.splice(i, 1);
            }
        }

        // D. Nodos de Aggro (Bombas y Cruces de Curación con paleta NieR)
        for (let i = this.nodes.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const n = this.nodes[i];
            n.timer -= dt;

            if (n.timer <= 0) {
                if (n.type === 'bomb') {
                    ParticleEngine.explode(n.x, n.y, '#d9381e', 24);
                    if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.playAudio(BOSS_AUDIO_PATHS.bombExplode);
                    this.spawnDanmakuRing(n.x, n.y, 12, 3.3, 'ring');
                } else if (n.type === 'cross') {
                    BossState.currentHp = Math.min(BossState.maxHp, BossState.currentHp + 40);
                    BossState.updateHpBar();
                    BossState.spawnDamagePopup(core.x, core.y, "CURACIÓN +40", 'heal');
                    ParticleEngine.explode(n.x, n.y, '#e5c158', 20);
                    if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.beep(660, 0.25, 'sine', 0.06);

                    ctx.save();
                    ctx.strokeStyle = '#e5c158';
                    ctx.lineWidth = 6;
                    ctx.shadowColor = '#f7f5ed';
                    ctx.shadowBlur = 20;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.moveTo(n.x, n.y);
                    ctx.lineTo(core.x, core.y);
                    ctx.stroke();
                    ctx.restore();
                }
                this.nodes.splice(i, 1);
                continue;
            }

            ctx.save();
            ctx.translate(n.x, n.y);
            const timeRatio = Math.max(0, n.timer / n.maxTimer);
            ctx.strokeStyle = n.type === 'bomb' ? '#d9381e' : '#e5c158';
            ctx.lineWidth = 3;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(0, 0, n.radius + 6, -Math.PI / 2, -Math.PI / 2 + Math.PI * 2 * timeRatio);
            ctx.stroke();

            if (n.type === 'bomb') {
                ctx.fillStyle = '#1a0d0a';
                ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff9e80';
                ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
                ctx.shadowColor = '#d9381e';
                ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.arc(0, 0, n.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                ctx.fill();
                ctx.stroke();

                ctx.fillStyle = '#f7f5ed';
                ctx.font = 'bold 11px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
                ctx.textAlign = 'center';
                ctx.fillText(n.timer.toFixed(1), 0, 4);
            } else {
                ctx.save();
                ctx.rotate(performance.now() * 0.0025);
                ctx.fillStyle = '#e5c158';
                ctx.shadowColor = '#f7f5ed';
                ctx.shadowBlur = 16;
                ctx.fillRect(-n.radius, -5, n.radius * 2, 10);
                ctx.fillRect(-5, -n.radius, 10, n.radius * 2);
                ctx.restore();
            }

            if (n.hp < n.maxHp) {
                const w = 42;
                ctx.fillStyle = '#09090b';
                ctx.fillRect(-w / 2, -n.radius - 16, w, 5);
                ctx.fillStyle = '#dad4bb';
                ctx.fillRect(-w / 2, -n.radius - 16, w * (n.hp / n.maxHp), 5);
            }
            ctx.restore();
        }

        // E. Láseres Telegráficos YoRHa
        for (let i = this.lasers.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const l = this.lasers[i];
            l.timer += dt;

            ctx.save();
            ctx.translate(l.x, l.y);
            ctx.rotate(l.angle);

            if (l.state === 'warn') {
                const alpha = 0.5 + Math.sin(l.timer * 28) * 0.35;
                ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(217, 56, 30, ${alpha})`;
                ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
                ctx.setLineDash([14, 8]);
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.moveTo(-window.innerWidth, 0);
                ctx.lineTo(window.innerWidth, 0);
                ctx.stroke();

                if (l.timer >= l.warnDuration) {
                    l.state = 'fire';
                    l.timer = 0;
                    if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.swordImpactSound();
                    if (typeof ScreenUtils !== 'undefined') ScreenUtils.triggerShake();
                }
            } else if (l.state === 'fire') {
                const width = 34 * (1 - l.timer / l.fireDuration);
                ctx.fillStyle = '#f7f5ed';
                ctx.shadowColor = '#d9381e';
                ctx.shadowBlur = 24;
                ctx.fillRect(-window.innerWidth, -width / 2, window.innerWidth * 2, width);

                if (!l.hasDamaged) {
                    const dx = BossState.shipX - l.x;
                    const dy = BossState.shipY - l.y;
                    const perpDist = Math.abs(-Math.sin(l.angle) * dx + Math.cos(l.angle) * dy);
                    if (perpDist < width * 0.65 + 12) {
                        l.hasDamaged = true;
                        BossState.tryHitPlayer(BossState.shipX, BossState.shipY);
                    }
                }
                if (l.timer >= l.fireDuration) this.lasers.splice(i, 1);
            }
            ctx.restore();
        }

        // F. Esferas Danmaku YoRHa
        for (let i = this.bullets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const b = this.bullets[i];
            b.x += b.vx * (dt * 60);
            b.y += b.vy * (dt * 60);

            if (b.x < -50 || b.x > window.innerWidth + 50 || b.y < -50 || b.y > window.innerHeight + 50) {
                this.bullets.splice(i, 1);
                continue;
            }

            ctx.save();
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.radius + 2.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(6, 6, 8, 0.88)';
            ctx.fill();

            ctx.shadowColor = b.ringColor;
            ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fillStyle = b.coreColor;
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.lineWidth = 2.2;
            ctx.strokeStyle = b.ringColor;
            ctx.stroke();

            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.radius * 0.38, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fillStyle = b.centerColor || '#f7f5ed';
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.restore();

            const distToShip = Math.hypot(b.x - BossState.shipX, b.y - BossState.shipY);
            if (BossState.shieldActive && distToShip < b.radius + 36) {
                ParticleEngine.explode(b.x, b.y, '#dad4bb', 5);
                this.bullets.splice(i, 1);
                continue;
            }

            if (distToShip < b.radius + 11) {
                this.bullets.splice(i, 1);
                BossState.tryHitPlayer(b.x, b.y);
            }
        }
    },

    // --- 5. CLÍMAX FASE 3: ESPADAS DESDE LAS ESQUINAS + EMBESTIDAS DE SAPHYNIEL ---
    startSurvivalMode() {
        clearInterval(this.survivalTimer);
        const surviveText = document.getElementById('survive-text');
        if (surviveText) {
            surviveText.classList.remove('show');
            void surviveText.offsetWidth;
            surviveText.classList.add('show');
            setTimeout(() => surviveText.classList.remove('show'), 3000);
        }

        let tick = 0;
        this.survivalTimer = setInterval(() => {
            if (BossState.phase !== 3 || BossState.playerHp <= 0 || BossState.damageLock) {
                clearInterval(this.survivalTimer);
                return;
            }
            tick++;

            // 1. Disparar espadas (swordsprite.png) desde las esquinas que se clavan en Saphyniel
            this.spawnJudgmentSword(tick % 4, 15);
            if (tick % 2 === 0) {
                setTimeout(() => this.spawnJudgmentSword((tick + 2) % 4, 15), 250);
            }

            // 2. Ataques desde la posición real del GIF de Saphyniel
            const core = BossState.getBossCoreCenter();
            this.spawnDanmakuRing(core.x, core.y, 10, 3.3, tick % 2 === 0 ? 'ring' : 'aimed');

            // 3. Cada 2 ciclos, Saphyniel ejecuta su Embestida Física (Tackle) hacia tu nave
            if (tick % 2 === 1) {
                BossState.startBossTackle();
            }

            // 4. Generar Orbes de Fe dorados para recargar el escudo
            if (tick % 3 === 0) {
                this.spawnFaithOrb();
            }
        }, 1250);
    },

    clearAll() {
        clearInterval(this.qteSpawnerTimer);
        clearInterval(this.bombSpawnerTimer);
        clearInterval(this.danmakuSpawnerTimer);
        clearInterval(this.laserSpawnerTimer);
        clearInterval(this.patternSpawnerTimer);
        clearInterval(this.survivalTimer);

        this.clearClones();
        this.bullets = [];
        this.lasers = [];
        this.nodes = [];
        this.faithOrbs = [];
        this.judgmentSwords = [];
    }
};

window.BossAttacks = BossAttacks;

    playFlashback() {
        const canvas = document.getElementById('flashback-canvas'); 
        if (!canvas) return;
        canvas.style.opacity = 1;
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d'); 
        canvas.width = window.innerWidth; 
        canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
        const history = BossState.history; 
        if (!history || history.length === 0) return;

        const maxTime = history[history.length - 1].time || 1000, duration = 12450;
        const scale = maxTime / duration, startRealTime = performance.now();
        const sprite = new Image(); 
        sprite.src = BOSS_SPRITES.corePhase1_2;

        const drawLoop = (now) => {
            if (BossState.phase !== 3) return;
            const elapsed = now - startRealTime;
            if (elapsed > duration) { 
                canvas.style.opacity = 0; 
                ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height); 
                return; 
            }

            const targetTime = elapsed * scale;
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.18)'; 
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
            const events = history.filter(h => h.time <= targetTime && !h.drawn);
            events.forEach(h => {
                h.drawn = true;
                if (h.type === 'move') {
                    const x = (h.x / 100) * canvas.width, y = (h.y / 100) * canvas.height;
                    ctx.save(); 
                    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.6; 
                    ctx.translate(x, y); 
                    ctx.drawImage(sprite, -40, -40, 80, 80); 
                    ctx.restore();
                } else if (h.type === 'hit') {
                    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9)'; 
                    ctx.beginPath(); 
                    ctx.arc(h.x, h.y, 35, 0, Math.PI * 2); 
                    ctx.fill();
                }
            });
            requestAnimationFrame(drawLoop);
        };
        requestAnimationFrame(drawLoop);
    },

            if (AudioManager.bgmTrack) AudioManager.bgmTrack.pause();

            let cinematicPhase = 0, lastCountdown = 0;
            const countdownTimes = [ { t: 141.2, txt: "1" }, { t: 142.0, txt: "2" }, { t: 142.8, txt: "1" }, { t: 143.4, txt: "2" }, { t: 144.0, txt: "3" }, { t: 144.6, txt: "4" } ];

            const directorLoop = () => {
                if (BossState.phase !== 3 || BossState.playerHp <= 0) return;

                const ct = bgm.currentTime;

                        BossState.updateHpBar();

                        if (Math.random() < 0.12) ScreenUtils.triggerShake();

            this.subtitleLoopId = requestAnimationFrame(directorLoop);

            const startPhase3Audio = () => {
                bgm.currentTime = 99.0; 
                bgm.volume = 0.05; 
                bgm.play().catch(e => console.warn(e));
                document.body.classList.add('sepia-melancholy');
                let curVol = 0.05;
                const fadeIn = setInterval(() => { 
                    curVol = Math.min(0.65, curVol + 0.04); 
                    if (bgm) bgm.volume = curVol; 
                    if (curVol >= 0.65) clearInterval(fadeIn); 
                }, 80);
            };

            if (bgm.readyState >= 1) startPhase3Audio(); 
            else bgm.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', startPhase3Audio, { once: true });
        }, 1000);
    },

    startPhase3CameraFX() {
        clearInterval(this.phase3CameraInterval); 
        let step = 0;
        this.phase3CameraInterval = setInterval(() => {
            document.body.classList.remove('phase-3-tilt-a', 'phase-3-tilt-b', 'phase-3-tilt-reset');
            if (step === 0) { document.body.classList.add('phase-3-tilt-a'); step = 1; } 
            else if (step === 1) { document.body.classList.add('phase-3-tilt-reset'); step = 2; } 
            else if (step === 2) { document.body.classList.add('phase-3-tilt-b'); step = 3; } 
            else { document.body.classList.add('phase-3-tilt-reset'); step = 0; }
        }, 1200); 
    },

    triggerDefeatDialogueSequence() {
        document.body.classList.remove('phase-3-active', 'phase-3-tilt-a', 'phase-3-tilt-b', 'phase-3-tilt-reset', 'hyper-flashback', 'sepia-melancholy', 'glitch-flash', 'phase-3-shatter-zoom');
        
        const lyrics = document.getElementById('lyrics-container');
        if (lyrics) lyrics.innerHTML = '';

        AudioManager.playBgm(BOSS_AUDIO_PATHS.endingBgm, 0.7);

        const defeatLines = [
            { text: "Has borrado cada línea que me sostenía.", tone: "low", time: 4200 },
            { text: "Tantas estructuras... tantas leyes que creí eternas...", tone: "low", time: 4200 },
            { text: "Supongo que al final no se trataba de ser fuerte, sino de saber cuándo dejar ir.", tone: "mid", time: 4500 },
            { text: "Gracias por quedarte. Aunque haya sido para ver cómo caía todo.", tone: "mid", time: 4500 }
        ];

        let dIdx = 0;
        const playNextLine = () => {
            if (dIdx < defeatLines.length) {
                const cur = defeatLines[dIdx];
                DialogueSystem.show({
                    text: cur.text,
                    speaker: "SAPHYNIEL",
                    tone: cur.tone,
                    autoHideTime: cur.time,
                    spriteMap: BOSS_SPRITES,
                    callback: () => {
                        dIdx++;
                        setTimeout(playNextLine, 350);
                    }
                });
            } else {
                DialogueSystem.hide();
                const p5 = document.getElementById('p5-box');
                if (p5) p5.style.display = 'none';

                const endScreen = document.getElementById('ending-screen');
                if (endScreen) {
                    endScreen.style.display = 'flex';
                    void endScreen.offsetWidth;
                    endScreen.classList.add('visible');
                }

                triggerCreditsSequence(() => {
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        const bellEl = document.getElementById('end-record-final');
                        if (bellEl) {
                            bellEl.textContent = "SAPHYNIEL //";
                            bellEl.classList.add('active');
                            setTimeout(() => {
                                bellEl.classList.remove('active'); 
                                if (endScreen) endScreen.classList.remove('visible');
                                window.parent.postMessage({ type: 'BOSS_DEFEATED' }, window.location.origin === 'null' || window.location.protocol === 'file:' ? '*' : window.location.origin);
                            }, 4500);
                        }
                    }, 2000);
                });
            }
        };

        setTimeout(playNextLine, 600);
    }
};

=== FILE: js/boss/fsm/boss-engine.js ===
// =========================================================
// MÁQUINA DE ESTADOS FINITOS (FSM) SINCRONIZADA AL CANVAS
// =========================================================
const BossEngine = {
    currentPhase: null,
    phases: {},
    lastFrameTime: 0,

    register(name, phaseInstance) {
        this.phases[name] = phaseInstance;
    },

    startLoop() {
        this.lastFrameTime = performance.now();
        if (typeof ParticleEngine !== 'undefined') {
            ParticleEngine.customOverlayRender = (ctx) => {
                const now = performance.now();
                const dt = Math.min(0.1, (now - this.lastFrameTime) / 1000);
                this.lastFrameTime = now;
                this.update(dt, now, ctx);
            };
        }
    },

    transitionTo(phaseName, ...args) {
        if (this.currentPhase && typeof this.currentPhase.exit === 'function') {
            this.currentPhase.exit();
        }
        const next = this.phases[phaseName];
        if (!next) return;
        this.currentPhase = next;
        if (typeof this.currentPhase.enter === 'function') {
            this.currentPhase.enter(...args);
        }
        this.startLoop();
    },

    update(dt, now, ctx) {
        if (this.currentPhase && typeof this.currentPhase.update === 'function') {
            this.currentPhase.update(dt, now);
        }
        if (ctx) {
            BossState.updateAndRenderShip(dt, ctx);
            BossAttacks.updateProjectilesAndLasers(dt, ctx);
        }
    },

    handleHit(dmg, coords) {
        if (this.currentPhase && typeof this.currentPhase.onHit === 'function') {
            return this.currentPhase.onHit(dmg, coords);
        }
        return true;
    },

    checkDebugParams() {
        const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        return params.get('phase');
    }
};

window.BossEngine = BossEngine;
=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: js/boss/phases/phase-1.js ===
const Phase1 = {
    hit75: false,
    hit50: false,
    hit20: false,
    alertBannerTimer: 0,

    enter() {
        BossState.phase = 1;
        BossState.maxHp = 480;
        BossState.currentHp = 480;
        BossState.damageLock = true;
        BossState.isInvulnerable = false;
        BossState.weaponLost = false;

        this.hit75 = false;
        this.hit50 = false;
        this.hit20 = false;
        this.alertBannerTimer = 2.6;

        BossState.updateHpBar();
        BossState.updateCoreSprite();

        const title = document.getElementById('boss-title-name');
        if (title) title.textContent = "SAPHYNIEL // DEIDAD TRANSCENDENTE";

        document.body.className = 'combat-no-cursor';
        document.getElementById('cinematic-darkness')?.classList.remove('active');

        if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') {
            AudioManager.init();
            AudioManager.playBgm(BOSS_AUDIO_PATHS.bgmPhase1, 0.48, true);
            AudioManager.playAudio(BOSS_AUDIO_PATHS.enrageAlarm, 0.35);
        }

        const startCombatTimers = () => {
            BossState.damageLock = false;
            BossAttacks.qteSpawnerTimer = setInterval(() => BossAttacks.spawnQteCross(), 6400);
            BossAttacks.danmakuSpawnerTimer = setInterval(() => {
                if (!BossState.damageLock) {
                    const core = BossState.getBossCoreCenter();
                    BossAttacks.spawnDanmakuRing(core.x, core.y, 10, 3.2, 'aimed');
                }
            }, 3100);
            // Variedad extra en Fase 1: Espiral de Relojería + Ráfaga Francotirador
            let pStep = 0;
            BossAttacks.patternSpawnerTimer = setInterval(() => {
                if (BossState.damageLock) return;
                pStep++;
                if (pStep % 3 === 0) BossAttacks.spawnAimedBurst();
                else BossAttacks.spawnSpiralVolley(3, 2, 3.1);
            }, 1300);
        };

        if (BossEngine.checkDebugParams() === '1') {
            startCombatTimers();
            return;
        }

        DialogueSystem.show({
            text: "Has forzado la puerta de mi santuario. [WASD: MOVER | ESPACIO: ESCUDO | E: RAILGUN]",
            speaker: "SAPHYNIEL // DEIDAD",
            tone: "angry",
            autoHideTime: 2600,
            spriteMap: BOSS_SPRITES,
            callback: () => {
                if (typeof AudioManager !== 'undefined') AudioManager.playAudio(BOSS_AUDIO_PATHS.bossIntro);
                startCombatTimers();
            }
        });
    },

    update(dt) {
        if (this.alertBannerTimer > 0 && typeof ParticleEngine !== 'undefined' && ParticleEngine.ctx) {
            this.alertBannerTimer -= dt;
            const ctx = ParticleEngine.ctx;
            const w = window.innerWidth;
            const h = window.innerHeight;
            ctx.save();
            ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(217, 56, 30, ${Math.min(0.85, this.alertBannerTimer * 0.6)})`;
            ctx.fillRect(0, h * 0.44, w, 46);
            ctx.fillStyle = '#f7f5ed';
            ctx.font = 'bold 14px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
            ctx.textAlign = 'center';
            ctx.fillText('⚠️ ALERTA YORHA // ENTIDAD DIVINA HOSTIL DETECTADA ⚠️', w / 2, h * 0.44 + 29);
            ctx.restore();
        }
    },

    onHit(dmg, coords) {
        if (BossState.currentHp <= 1) {
            BossState.currentHp = 1;
            BossState.updateHpBar();
            BossEngine.transitionTo('phase2');
            return false;
        }

        const hpPercent = (BossState.currentHp / BossState.maxHp) * 100;
        if (hpPercent <= 75 && !this.hit75) {
            this.hit75 = true;
            BossAttacks.spawnTelegraphLaser();
            DialogueSystem.show({ text: "No es un juego para mí. Es el único refugio que tengo en pie.", speaker: "SAPHYNIEL // DEIDAD", tone: "mid", autoHideTime: 2500, spriteMap: BOSS_SPRITES });
        } else if (hpPercent <= 50 && !this.hit50) {
            this.hit50 = true;
            BossAttacks.spawnBomb();
            BossAttacks.spawnQteCross();
            DialogueSystem.show({ text: "Persistes tanto... como si vencer aquí fuera a llenar el vacío fuera de la pantalla.", speaker: "SAPHYNIEL // DEIDAD", tone: "low", autoHideTime: 2800, spriteMap: BOSS_SPRITES });
        } else if (hpPercent <= 20 && !this.hit20) {
            this.hit20 = true;
            BossAttacks.spawnLaserGrid(2);
            DialogueSystem.show({ text: "¿Qué esperas encontrar cuando este registro llegue a cero?", speaker: "SAPHYNIEL // DEIDAD", tone: "angry", autoHideTime: 2500, spriteMap: BOSS_SPRITES });
        }
        return true;
    },

    exit() {
        BossAttacks.clearAll();
    }
};
BossEngine.register('phase1', Phase1);
